# 🚀 CAREERFORGE Batch 18 Issues Catalog: 300 Multi-Level Contributor Tasks

**Repository:** `Pratyush-Panda-2006/CAREERFORGE`  
**Initiative:** Nexus Spring of Code 2026 (NSoC '26)  
**Batch Scope:** **Multi-Level Tasks Across All 40 Showcase Projects (Level 1, Level 2, and Level 3)**  
**Catalog Size:** 300 Handcrafted Showcase Enhancements (IDs #1301 to #1600)  
**Distribution:** **100 Level 1 Tasks (3 Points)** | **100 Level 2 Tasks (5 Points)** | **100 Level 3 Tasks (10 Points)**  
**Zero-Merge-Conflict Architecture:** Every single task operates in its own **dedicated, isolated files** (e.g. `[feature].html`, `[feature].css`, `[feature].js`, plus `[feature].test.mjs` for Level 3). Contributors **do not edit shared files** (`index.html`, `styles.css`, `script.js`), eliminating 100% of git merge conflicts!

## 1. Multi-Level Scoring & Guidelines

Batch 18 provides opportunities for contributors across all skill levels under Nexus Spring of Code 2026:

| Tier | Label | Points | Deliverable | Conflict Prevention Strategy |
| :---: | :---: | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **Level 1** | `level1` | **3 Points** | **Isolated UI Sub-View** | Build in dedicated HTML/CSS/JS without touching shared files. |
| **Level 2** | `level2` | **5 Points** | **Interactive Tool & Component** | Build dynamic tool, validator, or parser in dedicated files. |
| **Level 3** | `level3` | **10 Points** | **Isolated Engine & Test Suite** | Build complete engine with dedicated HTML, CSS, JS, and Unit Test. |

### 🛡️ Why Contributors Face ZERO Merge Conflicts:
- **No Shared File Contention:** Tasks do not modify existing shared files (`index.html`, `styles.css`, `script.js`).
- **Dedicated Unique Filenames:** Every task specifies its own dedicated files under `public/showcase/[project-slug]/`.
- **Instant Concurrent Merges:** Because each PR introduces new distinct files, dozens of contributors can submit and merge PRs concurrently with zero merge conflicts!

## 3. Detailed Task Specifications (300 Issues: #1301 to #1600)

#### 1301. `[ecommerce-store]` Implement dynamic breadcrumbs path navigator & history stack in dedicated modular files
- **Labels:** `nsoc26`, `level1`, `enhancement`, `good-first-issue`
- **Showcase Path:** `public/showcase/ecommerce-store/`
- **Points:** **3 Points** (Level 1 (3 Points))
- **Conflict-Free Guard:** Zero-Merge-Conflict Guarantee: This task is implemented strictly in its own isolated files (`breadcrumbs-navigator.html`, `breadcrumbs-navigator.css`, `breadcrumbs-navigator.js`). Do not modify common files (index.html, styles.css, script.js) to guarantee 100% conflict-free merging!
- **Description:** Build an isolated client-side breadcrumb navigation trail and recent view history stack for E-Commerce Store that tracks hierarchical route depth, displays interactive navigation crumb links, and supports instant traversal back to previous parent nodes.
- **Step-by-Step Implementation Guide:**
  - Dedicated HTML: Create `public/showcase/ecommerce-store/breadcrumbs-navigator.html` with interactive route segment trails, home icon anchor, and active page pill.
  - Dedicated CSS: Create `public/showcase/ecommerce-store/breadcrumbs-navigator.css` styling chevron separators, active state emphasis, and responsive wrapping for narrow screens.
  - Dedicated Module JS: Create `public/showcase/ecommerce-store/breadcrumbs-navigator.js` implementing class `EcommerceStoreBreadcrumbNav` with `pushRoute(segment, url)`, `popRoute()`, and `renderTrail()`.

#### 1307. `[ecommerce-store]` Implement token bucket / leaky bucket rate limiter & concurrency throttler in dedicated modular files
- **Labels:** `nsoc26`, `level3`, `enhancement`
- **Showcase Path:** `public/showcase/ecommerce-store/`
- **Points:** **10 Points** (Level 3 (10 Points))

Do not modify common files (index.html, styles.css, script.js) to guarantee 100% conflict-free merging!
- **Description:** Build an autonomous client-side Rate Limiter engine for E-Commerce Store implementing the Token Bucket algorithm with refill rate intervals, burst capacity limits, queue prioritization, and exhaustive automated unit tests.
- **Step-by-Step Implementation Guide:**
  - Dedicated HTML: Create `public/showcase/ecommerce-store/rate-limiter.html` with request flood simulator buttons, bucket fill level progress meter, and rate-limited event log stream.
  - Dedicated CSS: Create `public/showcase/ecommerce-store/rate-limiter.css` with liquid bucket reservoir animation, accepted/throttled status badges, and request telemetry metrics.
  - Dedicated Module JS: Create `public/showcase/ecommerce-store/rate-limiter.js` implementing class `EcommerceStoreTokenBucketRateLimiter` with `tryConsume(tokens)`, `refill()`, and `getAvailableTokens()`.
  - Isolated Unit Test: Create `public/showcase/ecommerce-store/rate-limiter.test.mjs` testing burst capacity bounds, time-based token replenishment accuracy, and request rejection under exhaustion with node:test.

#### 1308. `[ecommerce-store]` Implement least-recently-used (lru) in-memory cache with eviction visualizer in dedicated modular files
- **Labels:** `nsoc26`, `level3`, `enhancement`
- **Showcase Path:** `public/showcase/ecommerce-store/`
- **Points:** **10 Points** (Level 3 (10 Points))
- **Conflict-Free Guard:** Zero-Merge-Conflict Guarantee: This task is implemented strictly in its own isolated files (`lru-cache.html`, `lru-cache.css`, `lru-cache.js`, `lru-cache.test.mjs`). Do not modify common files (index.html, styles.css, script.js) to guarantee 100% conflict-free merging!
- **Description:** Build a doubly-linked-list and hash-map backed LRU (Least Recently Used) cache engine for E-Commerce Store that supports O(1) reads/writes, automatic eviction of oldest entries upon reaching capacity, and dynamic visual node state inspection.
- **Step-by-Step Implementation Guide:**
  - Dedicated HTML: Create `public/showcase/ecommerce-store/lru-cache.html` with key-value insertion form, cache capacity slider, eviction log, and ordered node chain diagram.
  - Dedicated CSS: Create `public/showcase/ecommerce-store/lru-cache.css` with animated node eviction fades, head/tail pointer highlight badges, and memory occupancy bars.
  - Dedicated Module JS: Create `public/showcase/ecommerce-store/lru-cache.js` implementing class `EcommerceStoreLruCache` with `get(key)`, `put(key, value)`, and `getEvictedEntries()`.
  - Isolated Unit Test: Create `public/showcase/ecommerce-store/lru-cache.test.mjs` asserting O(1) access updates, correct eviction of least recently used items on overflow, and cache clear resets with node:test.

  - Isolated Unit Test: Create `public/showcase/docs-portal/lru-cache.test.mjs` asserting O(1) access updates, correct eviction of least recently used items on overflow, and cache clear resets with node:test.

  - Dedicated Module JS: Create `public/showcase/luxury-realms/lru-cache.js` implementing class `LuxuryRealmsLruCache` with `get(key)`, `put(key, value)`, and `getEvictedEntries()`.
  - Isolated Unit Test: Create `public/showcase/luxury-realms/lru-cache.test.mjs` asserting O(1) access updates, correct eviction of least recently used items on overflow, and cache clear resets with node:test.

## 4. Automated Publishing & Dry-Run Guide

Batch 18 issues can be previewed or published to GitHub via the companion script:

```bash
# 1. Preview all 300 Batch 18 issues in dry-run mode
node scripts/create-batch18-issues.mjs --dry-run

# 2. Or using npm run script
npm run create-batch18-issues -- --dry-run

# 3. Publish directly to GitHub repository (via GitHub Actions or with GITHUB_TOKEN)
node scripts/create-batch18-issues.mjs
```
